class="font-medium text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-0">API</span></div><div class="prose prose-lg max-w-none"><h1 class="text-large mb-8">Origin Grants API</h1>
<div class="relative grid w-full grid-cols-[0_1fr] gap-y-0.5 rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-sm has-[&gt;svg]:grid-cols-[calc(var(--spacing)*4)_1fr] has-[&gt;svg]:gap-x-3 [&amp;&gt;svg]:size-4 [&amp;&gt;svg]:translate-y-0.5 [&amp;&gt;svg]:text-current text-card-foreground my-6 items-center border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-700 dark:bg-yellow-900/20" data-slot="alert" role="alert"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-triangle-alert h-4 w-4 mb-1 text-yellow-600 dark:text-yellow-400" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m21.73 18-8-14a2 2 0 0 0-3.48 0l-8 14A2 2 0 0 0 4 21h16a2 2 0 0 0 1.73-3"></path><path d="M12 9v4"></path><path d="M12 17h.01"></path></svg><div class="col-start-2 grid justify-items-start gap-1 text-sm [&amp;_p]:mb-0 [&amp;_p]:leading-relaxed text-yellow-700 dark:text-yellow-300" data-slot="alert-description"><p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Origin is in Early Beta and subject to change.</p></div></div>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A grant binds one principal to one repository or namespace with one permission. The grants API lists the grants held directly on a resource, upserts the grant a principal holds, and deletes it, so access changes can be scripted and reviewed as code. Writes reuse the access checks behind the Codebase permissions UI and record the same <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">repository.access_changed</code> and <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">namespace.access_changed</code> audit events.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">The six endpoints live in the <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#grants">Grants</a> group of the Origin API reference: <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#list-repository-grants">List Repository Grants</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#upsert-repository-grant">Upsert Repository Grant</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#delete-repository-grant">Delete Repository Grant</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#list-namespace-grants">List Namespace Grants</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#upsert-namespace-grant">Upsert Namespace Grant</a>, and <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#delete-namespace-grant">Delete Namespace Grant</a>. They share the <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin">Origin API</a> base URL, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#authentication">authentication</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#pagination">pagination</a>, and <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#errors">error model</a>. This page covers the concepts behind them.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="principals"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#principals" data-heading-base-id="principals" aria-label="Copy link to principals"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Principals</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Each grant names exactly one principal.</p>
<div class="my-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-sm border dark:border-muted"><table class="min-w-full divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-card dark:divide-muted dark:bg-card"><thead class="dark:text-neutral-300"><tr><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Principal</th><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Field</th><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Identifies</th></tr></thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-background dark:divide-muted"><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">user</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">user.id</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">A Cursor user, by the encoded <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">user_…</code> id the organization API uses.</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">group</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">group.id</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">A Cursor group, by its public <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">grp_…</code> id: either a group the owner&#x27;s team owns, or a group in that team&#x27;s organization, returned as <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">publicId</code> by the <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/account/organizations/organization-admin-api#organization-groups">Organization API</a> group routes. The <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">g_…</code> <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">id</code> those routes take is a different identifier.</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">teamGroup</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">teamGroup.kind</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">One of the owning team&#x27;s built-in groups: <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">members</code> (every team member) or <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">admins</code> (team admins).</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A team-group grant is the floor every member of that built-in group holds on the resource. On a namespace it is the team&#x27;s namespace floor. On a repository it is the team&#x27;s per-repository override, and deleting it returns the repository to the namespace floor.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Users must belong to the owner&#x27;s organization. A group must be one the owner&#x27;s team owns, or an active group in that team&#x27;s organization; a team&#x27;s own groups are grantable even when the team is not linked to an organization. The write endpoints answer a user or group that does not exist exactly like one outside the organization, so a response never confirms that a principal exists. List responses omit principals that no longer resolve to an active user, group, or the owning team.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="permissions"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#permissions" data-heading-base-id="permissions" aria-label="Copy link to permissions"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Permissions</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Repository grants and namespace grants use different permission ladders. Both map to the presets the Codebase permissions UI offers.</p>
<div class="my-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-sm border dark:border-muted"><table class="min-w-full divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-card dark:divide-muted dark:bg-card"><thead class="dark:text-neutral-300"><tr><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Resource</th><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">permission</code> values</th></tr></thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-background dark:divide-muted"><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Repository</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">read</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">write</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">admin</code></td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Namespace</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_READ</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_CONTRIBUTOR</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_WRITE</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_ADMIN</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_READ</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_CONTRIBUTOR</code>, and <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_WRITE</code> grant that level on the namespace&#x27;s internal repositories. <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_ADMIN</code> administers the namespace itself.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">List responses report <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">custom</code> (repository) or <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_CUSTOM</code> (namespace) for a grant that holds a custom policy. The upsert endpoints reject those values with <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">InvalidArgument</code> (HTTP 400); custom policies are outside the grants API.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="scopes"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#scopes" data-heading-base-id="scopes" aria-label="Copy link to scopes"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Scopes</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Four scopes cover the grants API, all of them listed in <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#scopes">Scopes</a>: <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">repository:settings:read</code> and <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">repository:settings:write</code> for repository grants, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">namespace:settings:read</code> and <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">namespace:settings:write</code> for namespace grants.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">App installations can hold all four, so a robot calls the grants API with an installation access token. User access tokens work too. Requesting a <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">:write</code> scope also grants the matching <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">:read</code> scope. List endpoints cost 1 point and writes cost 5 against the budget in <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#rate-limits">Rate limits</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="upserts-and-deletes"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#upserts-and-deletes" data-heading-base-id="upserts-and-deletes" aria-label="Copy link to upserts-and-deletes"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Upserts and deletes</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Upsert is a <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">POST</code> that creates or replaces the grant a principal holds on the resource. Each principal holds one grant per resource, so repeating a request leaves the same grant in place and a different <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">permission</code> replaces the earlier one. Delete names the principal in the request body and returns <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">204 No Content</code>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A namespace always keeps at least one admin. An upsert or delete that would leave the owner without one returns <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">FailedPrecondition</code> (HTTP 400).</p></div><div class="font-medium text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-0">API</span></div><div class="prose prose-lg max-w-none"><h1 class="text-large mb-8">Origin Grants API</h1>
<div class="relative grid w-full grid-cols-[0_1fr] gap-y-0.5 rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-sm has-[&gt;svg]:grid-cols-[calc(var(--spacing)*4)_1fr] has-[&gt;svg]:gap-x-3 [&amp;&gt;svg]:size-4 [&amp;&gt;svg]:translate-y-0.5 [&amp;&gt;svg]:text-current text-card-foreground my-6 items-center border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-700 dark:bg-yellow-900/20" data-slot="alert" role="alert"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-triangle-alert h-4 w-4 mb-1 text-yellow-600 dark:text-yellow-400" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m21.73 18-8-14a2 2 0 0 0-3.48 0l-8 14A2 2 0 0 0 4 21h16a2 2 0 0 0 1.73-3"></path><path d="M12 9v4"></path><path d="M12 17h.01"></path></svg><div class="col-start-2 grid justify-items-start gap-1 text-sm [&amp;_p]:mb-0 [&amp;_p]:leading-relaxed text-yellow-700 dark:text-yellow-300" data-slot="alert-description"><p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Origin is in Early Beta and subject to change.</p></div></div>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A grant binds one principal to one repository or namespace with one permission. The grants API lists the grants held directly on a resource, upserts the grant a principal holds, and deletes it, so access changes can be scripted and reviewed as code. Writes reuse the access checks behind the Codebase permissions UI and record the same <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">repository.access_changed</code> and <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">namespace.access_changed</code> audit events.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">The six endpoints live in the <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#grants">Grants</a> group of the Origin API reference: <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#list-repository-grants">List Repository Grants</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#upsert-repository-grant">Upsert Repository Grant</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#delete-repository-grant">Delete Repository Grant</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#list-namespace-grants">List Namespace Grants</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#upsert-namespace-grant">Upsert Namespace Grant</a>, and <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#delete-namespace-grant">Delete Namespace Grant</a>. They share the <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin">Origin API</a> base URL, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#authentication">authentication</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#pagination">pagination</a>, and <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#errors">error model</a>. This page covers the concepts behind them.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="principals"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#principals" data-heading-base-id="principals" aria-label="Copy link to principals"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Principals</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Each grant names exactly one principal.</p>
<div class="my-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-sm border dark:border-muted"><table class="min-w-full divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-card dark:divide-muted dark:bg-card"><thead class="dark:text-neutral-300"><tr><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Principal</th><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Field</th><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Identifies</th></tr></thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-background dark:divide-muted"><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">user</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">user_…</code> id the organization API uses.</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">group</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">group.id</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">A Cursor group, by its public <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">grp_…</code> id: either a group the owner&#x27;s team owns, or a group in that team&#x27;s organization, returned as <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">publicId</code> by the <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/account/organizations/organization-admin-api#organization-groups">Organization API</a> group routes. The <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">g_…</code> <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">id</code> those routes take is a different identifier.</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">teamGroup</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">teamGroup.kind</code></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">One of the owning team&#x27;s built-in groups: <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">members</code> (every team member) or <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">admins</code> (team admins).</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A team-group grant is the floor every member of that built-in group holds on the resource. On a namespace it is the team&#x27;s namespace floor. On a repository it is the team&#x27;s per-repository override, and deleting it returns the repository to the namespace floor.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Users must belong to the owner&#x27;s organization. A group must be one the owner&#x27;s team owns, or an active group in that team&#x27;s organization; a team&#x27;s own groups are grantable even when the team is not linked to an organization. The write endpoints answer a user or group that does not exist exactly like one outside the organization, so a response never confirms that a principal exists. List responses omit principals that no longer resolve to an active user, group, or the owning team.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="permissions"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#permissions" data-heading-base-id="permissions" aria-label="Copy link to permissions"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Permissions</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Repository grants and namespace grants use different permission ladders. Both map to the presets the Codebase permissions UI offers.</p>
<div class="my-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-sm border dark:border-muted"><table class="min-w-full divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-card dark:divide-muted dark:bg-card"><thead class="dark:text-neutral-300"><tr><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Resource</th><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">permission</code> values</th></tr></thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-background dark:divide-muted"><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Repository</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">read</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">write</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">admin</code></td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Namespace</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_READ</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_CONTRIBUTOR</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_WRITE</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_ADMIN</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_READ</code>, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_CONTRIBUTOR</code>, and <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_WRITE</code> grant that level on the namespace&#x27;s internal repositories. <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_ADMIN</code> administers the namespace itself.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">List responses report <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">custom</code> (repository) or <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">PERMISSION_CUSTOM</code> (namespace) for a grant that holds a custom policy. The upsert endpoints reject those values with <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">InvalidArgument</code> (HTTP 400); custom policies are outside the grants API.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="scopes"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#scopes" data-heading-base-id="scopes" aria-label="Copy link to scopes"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Scopes</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Four scopes cover the grants API, all of them listed in <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#scopes">Scopes</a>: <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">repository:settings:read</code> and <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">repository:settings:write</code> for repository grants, <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">namespace:settings:read</code> and <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">namespace:settings:write</code> for namespace grants.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">App installations can hold all four, so a robot calls the grants API with an installation access token. User access tokens work too. Requesting a <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">:write</code> scope also grants the matching <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">:read</code> scope. List endpoints cost 1 point and writes cost 5 against the budget in <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/api/origin#rate-limits">Rate limits</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="upserts-and-deletes"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#upserts-and-deletes" data-heading-base-id="upserts-and-deletes" aria-label="Copy link to upserts-and-deletes"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Upserts and deletes</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Upsert is a <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">POST</code> that creates or replaces the grant a principal holds on the resource. Each principal holds one grant per resource, so repeating a request leaves the same grant in place and a different <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">permission</code> replaces the earlier one. Delete names the principal in the request body and returns <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">204 No Content</code>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A namespace always keeps at least one admin. An upsert or delete that would leave the owner without one returns <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">FailedPrecondition</code> (HTTP 400).</p></div><div
